     *
     * @param str
     *            the percent encoded <code>String</code>
     * @return expanded form of the input, for example "foo%20bar" becomes
     *         "foo bar"
     */
    protected static String decodePercent(String str) {
        String decoded = null;
        try {
            decoded = URLDecoder.decode(str, "UTF8");
        } catch (UnsupportedEncodingException ignored) {
            NanoHTTPD.LOG.log(Level.WARNING, "Encoding not supported, ignored", ignored);
        }
        return decoded;
    }
    /**

     *         accespts it. Default this option is on for text content and off
     *         for everything. Override this for custom semantics.
     */
    protected boolean useGzipWhenAccepted(Response r) {
        return r.getMimeType() != null && r.getMimeType().toLowerCase().contains("text/");
    }

    public static Response newFixedLengthResponse(Response.IStatus status, String mimeType, InputStream data, long totalBytes) {

    }
    /**
     * Create a text response with known length.
     */
    public static Response newFixedLengthResponse(Response.IStatus status, String mimeType, String txt) {
        if (txt == null) {
            return newFixedLengthResponse(status, mimeType, new ByteArrayInputStream(new byte[0]), 0);
        } else {
            byte[] bytes;
            try {
                bytes = txt.getBytes("UTF-8");
            } catch (UnsupportedEncodingException e) {
                NanoHTTPD.LOG.log(Level.SEVERE, "encoding problem, responding nothing", e);
                bytes = new byte[0];
            }
            return newFixedLengthResponse(status, mimeType, new ByteArrayInputStream(bytes), bytes.length);
        }
    }
    /**
     * Create a text response with known length.
     */
    public static Response newFixedLengthResponse(String msg) {
        return newFixedLengthResponse(Response.Status.OK, NanoHTTPD.MIME_HTML, msg);
    }

    public Response serve(IHTTPSession session) {
        Map<String, String> files = new HashMap<String, String>();
        Method method = session.getMethod();
        if (Method.PUT.equals(method) || Method.POST.equals(method)) {
            try {
                session.parseBody(files);
            } catch (IOException ioe) {
                return newFixedLengthResponse(Response.Status.INTERNAL_ERROR, NanoHTTPD.MIME_PLAINTEXT, "SERVER INTERNAL ERROR: IOException: " + ioe.getMessage());
            } catch (ResponseException re) {
                return newFixedLengthResponse(re.getStatus(), NanoHTTPD.MIME_PLAINTEXT, re.getMessage());
            }
        }
        Map<String, String> parms = session.getParms();
        parms.put(NanoHTTPD.QUERY_STRING_PARAMETER, session.getQueryParameterString());
        return serve(session.getUri(), method, session.getHeaders(), parms, files);
    }

    /**
     * Start the server.
     *
     * @param timeout
     *            timeout to use for socket connections.
     * @param daemon
     *            start the thread daemon or not.
     * @throws IOException
     *             if the socket is in use.
     */
    public void start(final int timeout, boolean daemon) throws IOException {
        this.myServerSocket = this.getServerSocketFactory().create();
        this.myServerSocket.setReuseAddress(true);
        ServerRunnable serverRunnable = createServerRunnable(timeout);
        this.myThread = new Thread(serverRunnable);
        this.myThread.setDaemon(daemon);
        this.myThread.setName("NanoHttpd Main Listener");
        this.myThread.start();
        while (!serverRunnable.hasBinded && serverRunnable.bindException == null) {
            try {
                Thread.sleep(10L);
            } catch (Throwable e) {
                // on android this may not be allowed, that's why we
                // catch throwable the wait should be very short because we are
                // just waiting for the bind of the socket
            }
        }
        if (serverRunnable.bindException != null) {
            throw serverRunnable.bindException;
        }
    }
    /**
     * Stop the server.
     */
    public void stop() {
        try {
            safeClose(this.myServerSocket);
            this.asyncRunner.closeAll();
            if (this.myThread != null) {
                this.myThread.join();
            }
        } catch (Exception e) {
            NanoHTTPD.LOG.log(Level.SEVERE, "Could not stop all connections", e);
        }
    }
    public final boolean wasStarted() {
        return this.myServerSocket != null && this.myThread != null;
    }

    /*
     * URL format: http://'hostname':'port'/stream?file='file_index'&torrent='torrent_hash'
     */

    public static String makeStreamUrl(@NonNull String hostname, int port,
                                       @NonNull String torrentId, int fileIndex)
    {
        try {
            return new URI("http", null, hostname, port,
                           "/stream",
                           String.format(Locale.getDefault(),
                                         "file=%d&torrent=%s",
                                         fileIndex,
                                         torrentId),
                           null)
                          .toString();
        } catch (URISyntaxException e) {
            return null;
        }
    }

    @Override
    public Response serve(IHTTPSession session)
    {
        String uri = session.getUri();
        String extension = uri.substring(uri.lastIndexOf('.') + 1);
        DLNAFileType fileType = DLNA_FILE_TYPES.get(extension);

        Response res = handleTorrent(session);
        if (fileType != null)
            fileType.setHeaders(res);

        return res;
    }

$ sed -n 123,252p TorrentStreamServer.java

    public Response handleTorrent(IHTTPSession httpSession)
    {
        if (engine == null)
            return newFixedLengthResponse(NOT_FOUND, "", "");

        if (!httpSession.getUri().equals("/stream"))
            return newFixedLengthResponse(BAD_REQUEST, "", "");

        Map<String, String> params = httpSession.getParms();
        if (params.size() < 2)
            return newFixedLengthResponse(BAD_REQUEST, "", "");

        String torrentId = Objects.requireNonNull(params.get("torrent"));

        int fileIndex;
        TorrentStream stream;
        try {
            fileIndex = Integer.parseInt(Objects.requireNonNull(params.get("file")));
            stream = engine.getStream(torrentId, fileIndex);
            if (stream == null)
                return newFixedLengthResponse(NOT_FOUND, "", "");

        } catch (Exception e) {
            return newFixedLengthResponse(NOT_FOUND, "", "");
        }

        Map<String, String> header = httpSession.getHeaders();
        try {
            Response res;
            String etag = stream.id;
            long startFrom = 0;
            long endAt = -1;
            String range = header.get("range");

            if (range != null) {
                if (range.startsWith("bytes=")) {
                    range = range.substring("bytes=".length());
                    int minus = range.indexOf('-');
                    try {
                        if (minus > 0) {
                            startFrom = Long.parseLong(range.substring(0, minus));
                            endAt = Long.parseLong(range.substring(minus + 1));
                        }
                    } catch (NumberFormatException ignored) {

                    }
                }
            }

            /*
             * Get if-range header. If present, it must match etag or else we
             * should ignore the range request
             */
            String ifRange = header.get("if-range");
            boolean headerIfRangeMissingOrMatching = (ifRange == null || etag.equals(ifRange));

            String ifNoneMatch = header.get("if-none-match");
            boolean headerIfNoneMatchPresentAndMatching = ifNoneMatch != null &&
                    ("*".equals(ifNoneMatch) || ifNoneMatch.equals(etag));

            if (headerIfRangeMissingOrMatching && range != null &&
                startFrom >= 0 && startFrom < stream.fileSize) {
                /*
                 * Range request that matches current etag
                 * and the startFrom of the range is satisfiable
                 */
                if (headerIfNoneMatchPresentAndMatching) {
                    /*
                     * Range request that matches current etag
                     * and the startFrom of the range is satisfiable
                     * would return range from file respond with not-modified
                     */
                    res = newFixedLengthResponse(NOT_MODIFIED, MIME_OCTET_STREAM, "");
                    res.addHeader("ETag", etag);

                } else {
                    if (endAt < 0)
                        endAt = stream.fileSize - 1;
                    long newLen = endAt - startFrom + 1;
                    if (newLen < 0)
                        newLen = 0;

                    TorrentInputStream is = engine.getTorrentInputStream(stream);
                    is.skip(startFrom);

                    res = newFixedLengthResponse(PARTIAL_CONTENT, MIME_OCTET_STREAM, is, newLen);
                    res.addHeader("Accept-Ranges", "bytes");
                    res.addHeader("Content-Length", "" + newLen);
                    res.addHeader("Content-Range", "bytes " + startFrom + "-" + endAt + "/" + stream.fileSize);
                    res.addHeader("ETag", etag);
                    res.addHeader("Content-Disposition", "inline; filename=" + stream.id);
                }
            } else {
                if (headerIfRangeMissingOrMatching && range != null && startFrom >= stream.fileSize) {
                    /*
                     * Return the size of the file
                     * 4xx responses are not trumped by if-none-match
                     */
                    res = newFixedLengthResponse(RANGE_NOT_SATISFIABLE, NanoHTTPD.MIME_PLAINTEXT, "");
                    res.addHeader("Content-Range", "bytes */" + stream.fileSize);
                    res.addHeader("ETag", etag);

                } else if (range == null && headerIfNoneMatchPresentAndMatching) {
                    /*
                     * Full-file-fetch request would return entire file
                     * respond with not-modified
                     */
                    res = newFixedLengthResponse(NOT_MODIFIED, MIME_OCTET_STREAM, "");
                    res.addHeader("ETag", etag);

                } else if (!headerIfRangeMissingOrMatching && headerIfNoneMatchPresentAndMatching) {
                    /*
                     * Range request that doesn't match current etag
                     * would return entire (different) file respond with not-modified
                     */
                    res = newFixedLengthResponse(NOT_MODIFIED, MIME_OCTET_STREAM, "");
                    res.addHeader("ETag", etag);

                } else {
                    TorrentInputStream is = engine.getTorrentInputStream(stream);
                    res = newFixedLengthResponse(OK, MIME_OCTET_STREAM, is, stream.fileSize);
                    res.addHeader("Accept-Ranges", "bytes");
                    res.addHeader("Content-Length", "" + stream.fileSize);
                    res.addHeader("ETag", etag);
                    res.addHeader("Content-Disposition", "inline; filename=" + stream.id);
                }
            }

            return res;

package com.ernieyu.feedparser.mediarss;

import androidx.annotation.NonNull;

import java.util.List;

/**
 * Media RSS is a new RSS module that supplements the <enclosure> capabilities of RSS 2.0.
 * RSS enclosures are already being used to syndicate audio files and images.
 * Media RSS extends enclosures to handle other media types, such as short films or TV,
 * as well as provide additional metadata with the media.
 * Media RSS enables content publishers and bloggers to syndicate multimedia content
 * such as TV and video clips, movies, images and audio.
 */

public class MediaRss {
    private List<Content> content;
    private Hash hash;
    private List<PeerLink> peerLinks;

    public MediaRss(List<Content> content,
                    Hash hash,
                    List<PeerLink> peerLinks)
    {
        this.content = content;
        this.hash = hash;
        this.peerLinks = peerLinks;
    }

    public List<Content> getContent() {
        return content;
    }

    public Hash getHash() {
        return hash;
    }

    public List<PeerLink> getPeerLinks() {
        return peerLinks;
    }

    @NonNull
    @Override
    public String toString() {
        return "MediaRss{" +
            "content=" + content +
            ", hash=" + hash +
            '}';
    }
}
$ sed -n 1,81p mediarss/Hash.java
package com.ernieyu.feedparser.mediarss;

import androidx.annotation.NonNull;

/**
 * This is the hash of the binary media file.
 * It can appear multiple times as long as each instance is a different algo.
 * It has one optional attribute.
 *
 * <media:hash algo="md5">dfdec888b72151965a34b4b59031290a</media:hash>
 *
 * algo indicates the algorithm used to create the hash.
 * Possible values are "md5" and "sha-1". Default value is "md5".
 * It is an optional attribute.
 */

public class Hash {
    private String value;
    private String algorithm = "md5";

    public Hash(String value) {
        this.value = value;
    }

    /**
     * Returns a hash.
     * <p>
     *
     * @return a hash, <b>null</b> if none.
     */

    public String getValue() {
        return value;
    }

    /**
     * Returns the hash algorithm.
     * <p>
     *
     * @return the hash algorithm.
     * Possible values are "md5" and "sha-1". Default value is "md5".
     */

    public String getAlgorithm() {
        return algorithm;
    }

    public void setAlgorithm(String algorithm) {
        if (algorithm == null)
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Algorithm cannot be null");
        this.algorithm = algorithm;
    }

    @Override
    public boolean equals(Object o) {
        if (this == o) return true;
        if (o == null || getClass() != o.getClass()) return false;

        Hash that = (Hash) o;

        if (value != null ? !value.equals(that.value) : that.value != null)
            return false;
        return algorithm != null ? algorithm.equals(that.algorithm) : that.algorithm == null;
    }

    @Override
    public int hashCode() {
        int result = value != null ? value.hashCode() : 0;
        result = 31 * result + (algorithm != null ? algorithm.hashCode() : 0);
        return result;
    }

    @NonNull
    @Override
    public String toString() {
        return "MediaRssHash{" +
            "value='" + value + '\'' +
            ", algorithm='" + algorithm + '\'' +
            '}';
    }
}

package com.ernieyu.feedparser.mediarss;

import androidx.annotation.NonNull;

/**
 * Optional element for P2P link.
 *
 * <media:peerLink type="application/x-bittorrent" href="http://www.example.org/sampleFile.torrent" />
 */

public class PeerLink {
    private String href;
    private String type;

    public PeerLink(String href, String type) {
        this.href = href;
        this.type = type;
    }

    /**
     * Returns a peer link.
     * <p>
     *
     * @return a peer link, <b>null</b> if none.
     */

    public String getHref() {
        return href;
    }

    /**
     * Returns the peer link MIME type.
     * <p>
     *
     * @return the peer link MIME type, <b>null</b> if none.
     */

    public String getType() {
        return type;
    }

    @Override
    public boolean equals(Object o) {
        if (this == o) return true;
        if (o == null || getClass() != o.getClass()) return false;

        PeerLink that = (PeerLink) o;

        if (href != null ? !href.equals(that.href) : that.href != null)
            return false;
        return type != null ? type.equals(that.type) : that.type == null;
    }

    @Override
    public int hashCode() {
        int result = href != null ? href.hashCode() : 0;
        result = 31 * result + (type != null ? type.hashCode() : 0);
        return result;
    }

    @NonNull
    @Override
    public String toString() {
        return "MediaRssPeerLink{" +
            "href='" + href + '\'' +
            ", type='" + type + '\'' +
            '}';
    }
}
$ sed -n 1,98p impl/MediaRssParser.java
package com.ernieyu.feedparser.impl;

import com.ernieyu.feedparser.Element;
import com.ernieyu.feedparser.mediarss.Content;
import com.ernieyu.feedparser.mediarss.Hash;
import com.ernieyu.feedparser.mediarss.MediaRss;
import com.ernieyu.feedparser.mediarss.PeerLink;
import org.xml.sax.Attributes;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

/**
 * A parser for Media RSS namespace (http://search.yahoo.com/mrss)
 */

class MediaRssParser {
    private static final String MEDIA_RSS_NAMESPACE_URI = "http://search.yahoo.com/mrss";
    private static final String MEDIA_RSS_CONTENT = "content";
    private static final String MEDIA_RSS_HASH = "hash";
    private static final String MEDIA_RSS_PEER_LINK = "peerLink";

    private Rss2Item item;

    public MediaRssParser(Rss2Item item) {
        this.item = item;
    }

    public MediaRss parse()
    {
        MediaRssParser parser = new MediaRssParser(item);

        List<Content> content = parser.parseContent();
        Hash hash = parser.parseHash();
        List<PeerLink> peerLinks = parser.parsePeerLinks();

        return new MediaRss(content, hash, peerLinks);
    }

    public List<Content> parseContent() {
        ArrayList<Content> content = new ArrayList<Content>();
        List<Element> mediaElems = item.getElementList(MEDIA_RSS_CONTENT);

        for (Element mediaElement : mediaElems) {
            if (!isMediaRssElement(mediaElement))
                continue;

            Attributes attr = mediaElement.getAttributes();
            String url = attr.getValue("url");
            String type = attr.getValue("type");

            content.add(new Content(url, type));
        }

        return content;
    }

    public Hash parseHash() {
        Element mediaElement = item.getElement(MEDIA_RSS_HASH);
        if (mediaElement == null || !isMediaRssElement(mediaElement))
            return null;

        Attributes attr = mediaElement.getAttributes();
        String hash = mediaElement.getContent();
        if (hash == null)
            return null;

        Hash mediaHash = new Hash(hash);
        String algo = attr.getValue("algo");
        if (algo != null)
            mediaHash.setAlgorithm(algo);

        return mediaHash;
    }

    public List<PeerLink> parsePeerLinks() {
        ArrayList<PeerLink> peerLinks = new ArrayList<PeerLink>();
        List<Element> mediaElems = item.getElementList(MEDIA_RSS_PEER_LINK);

        for (Element mediaElement : mediaElems) {
            if (!isMediaRssElement(mediaElement))
                continue;

            Attributes attr = mediaElement.getAttributes();
            String href = attr.getValue("href");
            String type = attr.getValue("type");

            peerLinks.add(new PeerLink(href, type));
        }

        return peerLinks;
    }

    private boolean isMediaRssElement(Element element) {
        return element.getUri() != null &&
            element.getUri().startsWith(MEDIA_RSS_NAMESPACE_URI);
    }
}

    public List<FeedItem> getItems()
    {
        List<FeedItem> items = new ArrayList<>();
        if (feed == null)
            return items;

        for (Item item : feed.getItemList()) {
            List<String> links = item.getLinks();
            String articleUrl = getFirstNotNullLink(links);
            String downloadUrl = watchDownloadableLink(links);

            /* Find url with torrent/magnet */
            if (downloadUrl == null)
                downloadUrl = findDownloadUrl(item);

            Date pubDate = item.getPubDate();
            long pubDateTime = 0;
            if (pubDate != null)
                pubDateTime = pubDate.getTime();

            FeedItem feedItem = new FeedItem(feedChannel.id, downloadUrl,
                    articleUrl, item.getTitle(), pubDateTime);
            feedItem.fetchDate = System.currentTimeMillis();
            items.add(feedItem);
        }

        return items;
    }

    private String getFirstNotNullLink(List<String> links)
    {
        for (String link : links) {
            if (!TextUtils.isEmpty(link))
                return link;
        }

        return null;
    }

    private String watchDownloadableLink(List<String> links)
    {
        for (String link : links) {
            if (link == null)
                continue;

            if (isMagnetOrTorrent(link))
                return link;
        }

        return null;
    }

    private String findDownloadUrl(Item item)
    {
        /*
         * Watch tags in descending order of importance
         * (hashes in the last place)
         */
        String enclosureUrl = watchEnclosure(item);
        if (enclosureUrl != null)
            return enclosureUrl;

        String infoHash = watchTorrentInfoHash(item);
        if (infoHash != null)
            return infoHash;

        String mediaContentUrl = watchMediaContent(item);
        if (mediaContentUrl != null)
            return mediaContentUrl;

        String guid = watchGuid(item);
        return guid;
    }

    /*
     * Parse element like this:
     * <enclosure type="application/x-bittorrent" url="http://foo.com/bar.torrent"/>
     */

    private String watchEnclosure(Item item)
    {
        for (Enclosure enclosure : item.getEnclosures()) {
            if (enclosure == null)
                continue;

            String url = enclosure.getUrl();
            String type = enclosure.getType();
            if (isMagnetOrTorrent(url) || (type != null && type.equals(Utils.MIME_TORRENT)))
                return url;
        }

        return null;
    }

    /*
     * Parse element like this:
     * <media:content type="application/x-bittorrent" url="http://foo.com/bar.torrent"/>
     */

    private String watchMediaContent(Item item)
    {
        MediaRss media = item.getMediaRss();
        if (media == null)
            return null;

        List<Content> contentList = media.getContent();
        for (Content content : contentList) {
            if (content == null)
                continue;

            String url = content.getUrl();
            if (url == null)
                return watchMediaHash(media);
            String type = content.getType();
            if (isMagnetOrTorrent(url) || (type != null && type.equals(Utils.MIME_TORRENT)))
                return url;
        }

        return watchMediaHash(media);
    }

    /*
     * Parse element like this:
     * <media:hash algo="sha1">8c056e06fbc16d2a2be79cefbf3e4ddc15396abe</media:hash>
     */

    private String watchMediaHash(MediaRss media)
    {
        Hash hash = media.getHash();
        if (hash == null)
            return null;

        String hashStr = hash.getValue();
        if (hashStr == null)
            return null;
        String algo = hash.getAlgorithm();
        if (Utils.isHash(hashStr) && algo != null && algo.equalsIgnoreCase("sha1"))
            return Utils.normalizeMagnetHash(hashStr);

        return null;
    }

    /*
     * Parse element like this:
     * <torrent:infoHash>8c056e06fbc16d2a2be79cefbf3e4ddc15396abe</torrent:infoHash>
     */

    private String watchTorrentInfoHash(Item item)

    /*
     * Interval in milliseconds
     */

    public static void runPeriodicalRefreshFeeds(@NonNull Context appContext, long interval) {
        Data data = new Data.Builder()
                .putString(FeedFetcherWorker.TAG_ACTION, FeedFetcherWorker.ACTION_FETCH_ALL_CHANNELS)
                .build();
        PeriodicWorkRequest work = new PeriodicWorkRequest.Builder(FeedFetcherWorker.class,
                interval,
                TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS)
                .setInputData(data)
                .setConstraints(getRefreshFeedsConstraints(appContext))
                .addTag(SCHEDULER_WORK_PERIODICAL_REFRESH_FEEDS)
                .build();

        WorkManager.getInstance(appContext).enqueueUniquePeriodicWork(SCHEDULER_WORK_PERIODICAL_REFRESH_FEEDS,
                ExistingPeriodicWorkPolicy.REPLACE, work);
    }

        SettingsRepository pref = RepositoryHelper.getSettingsRepository(appContext);

    private void refreshChannel(long feedId)
    {
        if (feedId == -1)
            return;

        Data data = new Data.Builder()
                .putString(FeedFetcherWorker.TAG_ACTION, FeedFetcherWorker.ACTION_FETCH_CHANNEL)
                .putLong(FeedFetcherWorker.TAG_CHANNEL_ID, feedId)
                .putBoolean(FeedFetcherWorker.TAG_NO_AUTO_DOWNLOAD, mutableParams.isNotDownloadImmediately())
                .build();

        OneTimeWorkRequest work = new OneTimeWorkRequest.Builder(FeedFetcherWorker.class)
                .setInputData(data)
                .build();

        WorkManager.getInstance(getApplication()).enqueue(work);
    }
}

$ sed -n 56,64p Utils (lambda region)
    public static void startServiceBackground(@NonNull Context context, @NonNull Intent i) {
        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.O) {
            // Postpone the service start until the main thread is free to avoid ANR
            new Handler(Looper.getMainLooper())
                    .post(() -> context.startForegroundService(i));
        } else {
            context.startService(i);
        }
    }

    public static void enableBootReceiver(@NonNull Context context, boolean enable) {
        SettingsRepository pref = RepositoryHelper.getSettingsRepository(context);
        boolean schedulingStart = pref.enableSchedulingStart();
        boolean schedulingStop = pref.enableSchedulingShutdown();
        boolean autostart = pref.autostart();

$ sed -n 47,68p ui/addfeed/AddFeedActivity.java
    public void onCreate(@Nullable Bundle savedInstanceState)
    {
        setTheme(Utils.getTranslucentAppTheme(getApplicationContext()));
        super.onCreate(savedInstanceState);

        FragmentManager fm = getSupportFragmentManager();
        addFeedDialog = (AddFeedDialog)fm.findFragmentByTag(TAG_ADD_FEED_DIALOG);
        if (addFeedDialog == null) {
            Intent i = getIntent();
            if (i == null)
                return;

            if (ACTION_EDIT_FEED.equals(i.getAction())) {
                long feedId = i.getLongExtra(TAG_FEED_ID, -1);
                addFeedDialog = AddFeedDialog.newInstance(feedId);
            } else {
                addFeedDialog = AddFeedDialog.newInstance(getUriFromIntent());
            }

            addFeedDialog.show(fm, TAG_ADD_FEED_DIALOG);
        }
    }

    public Dialog onCreateDialog(Bundle savedInstanceState)
    {
        if (activity == null)
            activity = (AppCompatActivity)getActivity();

        ViewModelProvider provider = new ViewModelProvider(activity);
        viewModel = provider.get(AddFeedViewModel.class);
        dialogViewModel = provider.get(BaseAlertDialog.SharedViewModel.class);
        clipboardViewModel = provider.get(ClipboardDialog.SharedViewModel.class);

        FragmentManager fm = getChildFragmentManager();
        deleteFeedDialog = (BaseAlertDialog)fm.findFragmentByTag(TAG_DELETE_FEED_DIALOG);
        clipboardDialog = (ClipboardDialog)fm.findFragmentByTag(TAG_CLIPBOARD_DIALOG);

        long feedId = getArguments().getLong(TAG_FEED_ID, -1);
        Uri uri = getArguments().getParcelable(TAG_URI);
        /* Clean */
        getArguments().putLong(TAG_FEED_ID, -1);
        getArguments().putParcelable(TAG_URI, null);
        initParams(uri, feedId);

        LayoutInflater i = LayoutInflater.from(activity);
        binding = DataBindingUtil.inflate(i, R.layout.dialog_add_feed_channel, null, false);
        binding.setViewModel(viewModel);

        initLayoutView();

        binding.getRoot().getViewTreeObserver().addOnWindowFocusChangeListener(onFocusChanged);

        return alert;
    }

    private void addChannel()
    {

                    R.string.error_cannot_add_channel,
                    Toast.LENGTH_SHORT)

        }

    public boolean addChannel()

156:    public class ClientHandler implements Runnable {
157:        private final InputStream inputStream;
158:        private final Socket acceptSocket;
159:        private ClientHandler(InputStream inputStream, Socket acceptSocket) {
160:            this.inputStream = inputStream;
161:            this.acceptSocket = acceptSocket;
162:        }
163:        public void close() {
164:            safeClose(this.inputStream);
165:            safeClose(this.acceptSocket);
166:        }
167:        @Override
168:        public void run() {
169:            OutputStream outputStream = null;
170:            try {
171:                outputStream = this.acceptSocket.getOutputStream();
172:                TempFileManager tempFileManager = NanoHTTPD.this.tempFileManagerFactory.create();
173:                HTTPSession session = new HTTPSession(tempFileManager, this.inputStream, outputStream, this.acceptSocket.getInetAddress());
174:                while (!this.acceptSocket.isClosed()) {
175:                    session.execute();
176:                }
177:            } catch (Exception e) {
178:                // When the socket is closed by the client,
179:                // we throw our own SocketException
180:                // to break the "keep alive" loop above. If
181:                // the exception was anything other
182:                // than the expected SocketException OR a
183:                // SocketTimeoutException, print the
184:                // stacktrace
185:                if (!(e instanceof SocketException && "NanoHttpd Shutdown".equals(e.getMessage())) && !(e instanceof SocketTimeoutException)) {
186:                    NanoHTTPD.LOG.log(Level.FINE, "Communication with the client broken", e);
187:                }
188:            } finally {
189:                safeClose(outputStream);
190:                safeClose(this.inputStream);
191:                safeClose(this.acceptSocket);
192:                NanoHTTPD.this.asyncRunner.closed(this);
193:            }
194:        }
319:        public void closeAll() {
320:            // copy of the list for concurrency
321:            for (ClientHandler clientHandler : new ArrayList<ClientHandler>(this.running)) {
322:                clientHandler.close();
323:            }
1511:    private static final void safeClose(Object closeable) {
1512:        try {
1513:            if (closeable != null) {
1514:                if (closeable instanceof Closeable) {
1515:                    ((Closeable) closeable).close();
1516:                } else if (closeable instanceof Socket) {
1517:                    ((Socket) closeable).close();
1518:                } else if (closeable instanceof ServerSocket) {
1519:                    ((ServerSocket) closeable).close();
1520:                } else {
1521:                    throw new IllegalArgumentException("Unknown object to close");
1522:                }
1523:            }
1524:        } catch (IOException e) {
1525:            NanoHTTPD.LOG.log(Level.SEVERE, "Could not close", e);

$ grep -n '' NanoHTTPD.java | sed -n '196,300p;326,338p;1324,1361p;1557,1563p'
196:    public static class Cookie {
197:        public static String getHTTPTime(int days) {
198:            Calendar calendar = Calendar.getInstance();
199:            SimpleDateFormat dateFormat = new SimpleDateFormat("EEE, dd MMM yyyy HH:mm:ss z", Locale.US);
200:            dateFormat.setTimeZone(TimeZone.getTimeZone("GMT"));
201:            calendar.add(Calendar.DAY_OF_MONTH, days);
202:            return dateFormat.format(calendar.getTime());
203:        }
204:        private final String n, v, e;
205:        public Cookie(String name, String value) {
206:            this(name, value, 30);
207:        }
208:        public Cookie(String name, String value, int numDays) {
209:            this.n = name;
210:            this.v = value;
211:            this.e = getHTTPTime(numDays);
212:        }
213:        public Cookie(String name, String value, String expires) {
214:            this.n = name;
215:            this.v = value;
216:            this.e = expires;
217:        }
218:        public String getHTTPHeader() {
219:            String fmt = "%s=%s; expires=%s";
220:            return String.format(fmt, this.n, this.v, this.e);
221:        }
222:    }
223:    /**
224:     * Provides rudimentary support for cookies. Doesn't support 'path',
225:     * 'secure' nor 'httpOnly'. Feel free to improve it and/or add unsupported
226:     * features.
227:     *
228:     * @author LordFokas
229:     */
230:    public class CookieHandler implements Iterable<String> {
231:        private final HashMap<String, String> cookies = new HashMap<String, String>();
232:        private final ArrayList<Cookie> queue = new ArrayList<Cookie>();
233:        public CookieHandler(Map<String, String> httpHeaders) {
234:            String raw = httpHeaders.get("cookie");
235:            if (raw != null) {
236:                String[] tokens = raw.split(";");
237:                for (String token : tokens) {
238:                    String[] data = token.trim().split("=");
239:                    if (data.length == 2) {
240:                        this.cookies.put(data[0], data[1]);
241:                    }
242:                }
243:            }
244:        }
245:        /**
246:         * Set a cookie with an expiration date from a month ago, effectively
247:         * deleting it on the client side.
248:         *
249:         * @param name
250:         *            The cookie name.
251:         */
252:        public void delete(String name) {
253:            set(name, "-delete-", -30);
254:        }
255:        @NonNull
256:        @Override
257:        public Iterator<String> iterator() {
258:            return this.cookies.keySet().iterator();
259:        }
260:        /**
261:         * Read a cookie from the HTTP Headers.
262:         *
263:         * @param name
264:         *            The cookie's name.
265:         * @return The cookie's value if it exists, null otherwise.
266:         */
267:        public String read(String name) {
268:            return this.cookies.get(name);
269:        }
270:        public void set(Cookie cookie) {
271:            this.queue.add(cookie);
272:        }
273:        /**
274:         * Sets a cookie.
275:         *
276:         * @param name
277:         *            The cookie's name.
278:         * @param value
279:         *            The cookie's value.
280:         * @param expires
281:         *            How many days until the cookie expires.
282:         */
283:        public void set(String name, String value, int expires) {
284:            this.queue.add(new Cookie(name, value, Cookie.getHTTPTime(expires)));
285:        }
286:        /**
287:         * Internally used by the webserver to add all queued cookies into the
288:         * Response's HTTP Headers.
289:         *
290:         * @param response
291:         *            The Response object to which headers the queued cookies
292:         *            will be added.
293:         */
294:        public void unloadQueue(Response response) {
295:            for (Cookie cookie : this.queue) {
296:                response.addHeader("Set-Cookie", cookie.getHTTPHeader());
297:            }
298:        }
299:    }
300:    /**
326:        public void closed(ClientHandler clientHandler) {
327:            this.running.remove(clientHandler);

498:                if (inLine == null) {
499:                    return;
500:                }
501:                StringTokenizer st = new StringTokenizer(inLine);
502:                if (!st.hasMoreTokens()) {
503:                    throw new ResponseException(Response.Status.BAD_REQUEST, "BAD REQUEST: Syntax error. Usage: GET /example/file.html");
504:                }

506:                if (!st.hasMoreTokens()) {

514:                    uri = decodePercent(uri.substring(0, qmi));
515:                } else {

636:         */
637:        private void decodeParms(String parms, Map<String, String> p) {
638:            if (parms == null) {
639:                this.queryParameterString = "";
640:                return;
641:            }
642:            this.queryParameterString = parms;
643:            StringTokenizer st = new StringTokenizer(parms, "&");
644:            while (st.hasMoreTokens()) {
645:                String e = st.nextToken();
646:                int sep = e.indexOf('=');
647:                if (sep >= 0) {
648:                    p.put(decodePercent(e.substring(0, sep)).trim(), decodePercent(e.substring(sep + 1)));
649:                } else {
650:                    p.put(decodePercent(e).trim(), "");
651:                }
652:            }
655:        public void execute() throws IOException {
656:            Response r = null;
657:            try {
658:                // Read the first 8192 bytes.
659:                // The full header should fit in here.
660:                // Apache's default header limit is 8KB.
661:                // Do NOT assume that a single read will get the entire header
662:                // at once!
663:                byte[] buf = new byte[HTTPSession.BUFSIZE];
664:                this.splitbyte = 0;
665:                this.rlen = 0;
666:                int read = -1;
667:                this.inputStream.mark(HTTPSession.BUFSIZE);
668:                try {
669:                    read = this.inputStream.read(buf, 0, HTTPSession.BUFSIZE);
670:                } catch (Exception e) {
671:                    safeClose(this.inputStream);
672:                    safeClose(this.outputStream);
673:                    throw new SocketException("NanoHttpd Shutdown");
674:                }
675:                if (read == -1) {
676:                    // socket was been closed
677:                    safeClose(this.inputStream);
678:                    safeClose(this.outputStream);
679:                    throw new SocketException("NanoHttpd Shutdown");
680:                }
681:                while (read > 0) {
682:                    this.rlen += read;
683:                    this.splitbyte = findHeaderEnd(buf, this.rlen);
684:                    if (this.splitbyte > 0) {
685:                        break;
686:                    }
687:                    read = this.inputStream.read(buf, this.rlen, HTTPSession.BUFSIZE - this.rlen);
688:                }
689:                if (this.splitbyte < this.rlen) {
690:                    this.inputStream.reset();
691:                    this.inputStream.skip(this.splitbyte);
692:                }
693:                this.parms = new HashMap<String, String>();
694:                if (null == this.headers) {
695:                    this.headers = new HashMap<String, String>();
696:                } else {
697:                    this.headers.clear();
698:                }
699:                // Create a BufferedReader for parsing the header.
700:                BufferedReader hin = new BufferedReader(new InputStreamReader(new ByteArrayInputStream(buf, 0, this.rlen)));
701:                // Decode the header into parms and header java properties
702:                Map<String, String> pre = new HashMap<String, String>();
703:                decodeHeader(hin, pre, this.parms, this.headers);
704:                if (null != this.remoteIp) {
705:                    this.headers.put("remote-addr", this.remoteIp);
706:                    this.headers.put("http-client-ip", this.remoteIp);
707:                }
708:                this.method = Method.lookup(pre.get("method"));
709:                if (this.method == null) {
710:                    throw new ResponseException(Response.Status.BAD_REQUEST, "BAD REQUEST: Syntax error.");
711:                }
712:                this.uri = pre.get("uri");
713:                this.cookies = new CookieHandler(this.headers);
714:                String connection = this.headers.get("connection");
715:                boolean keepAlive = protocolVersion.equals("HTTP/1.1") && (connection == null || !connection.matches("(?i).*close.*"));
716:                // Ok, now do the serve()
717:                // TODO: long body_size = getBodySize();
718:                // TODO: long pos_before_serve = this.inputStream.totalRead()
719:                // (requires implementaion for totalRead())
720:                r = serve(this);
721:                // TODO: this.inputStream.skip(body_size -
722:                // (this.inputStream.totalRead() - pos_before_serve))
723:                if (r == null) {
724:                    throw new ResponseException(Response.Status.INTERNAL_ERROR, "SERVER INTERNAL ERROR: Serve() returned a null response.");
725:                } else {
726:                    String acceptEncoding = this.headers.get("accept-encoding");
727:                    this.cookies.unloadQueue(r);
728:                    r.setRequestMethod(this.method);
729:                    r.setGzipEncoding(useGzipWhenAccepted(r) && acceptEncoding != null && acceptEncoding.contains("gzip"));
730:                    r.setKeepAlive(keepAlive);
731:                    r.send(this.outputStream);
732:                }
733:                if (!keepAlive || "close".equalsIgnoreCase(r.getHeader("connection"))) {
734:                    throw new SocketException("NanoHttpd Shutdown");
735:                }

124:    public Response handleTorrent(IHTTPSession httpSession)
125:    {
126:        if (engine == null)
127:            return newFixedLengthResponse(NOT_FOUND, "", "");
128:
129:        if (!httpSession.getUri().equals("/stream"))
130:            return newFixedLengthResponse(BAD_REQUEST, "", "");
131:
132:        Map<String, String> params = httpSession.getParms();
133:        if (params.size() < 2)
134:            return newFixedLengthResponse(BAD_REQUEST, "", "");
135:
136:        String torrentId = Objects.requireNonNull(params.get("torrent"));
137:
138:        int fileIndex;
139:        TorrentStream stream;
140:        try {
141:            fileIndex = Integer.parseInt(Objects.requireNonNull(params.get("file")));
142:            stream = engine.getStream(torrentId, fileIndex);
143:            if (stream == null)
144:                return newFixedLengthResponse(NOT_FOUND, "", "");
145:
146:        } catch (Exception e) {
147:            return newFixedLengthResponse(NOT_FOUND, "", "");
148:        }
149:
150:        Map<String, String> header = httpSession.getHeaders();
151:        try {
152:            Response res;
153:            String etag = stream.id;
154:            long startFrom = 0;
155:            long endAt = -1;
156:            String range = header.get("range");
157:
158:            if (range != null) {
159:                if (range.startsWith("bytes=")) {
160:                    range = range.substring("bytes=".length());
161:                    int minus = range.indexOf('-');
162:                    try {
163:                        if (minus > 0) {
164:                            startFrom = Long.parseLong(range.substring(0, minus));
165:                            endAt = Long.parseLong(range.substring(minus + 1));
166:                        }
167:                    } catch (NumberFormatException ignored) {
168:
169:                    }
170:                }
171:            }
172:
173:            /*
174:             * Get if-range header. If present, it must match etag or else we
175:             * should ignore the range request
176:             */
177:            String ifRange = header.get("if-range");
178:            boolean headerIfRangeMissingOrMatching = (ifRange == null || etag.equals(ifRange));
179:
180:            String ifNoneMatch = header.get("if-none-match");
181:            boolean headerIfNoneMatchPresentAndMatching = ifNoneMatch != null &&
182:                    ("*".equals(ifNoneMatch) || ifNoneMatch.equals(etag));
183:
184:            if (headerIfRangeMissingOrMatching && range != null &&
185:                startFrom >= 0 && startFrom < stream.fileSize) {
186:                /*
187:                 * Range request that matches current etag
188:                 * and the startFrom of the range is satisfiable
189:                 */
190:                if (headerIfNoneMatchPresentAndMatching) {
191:                    /*
192:                     * Range request that matches current etag
193:                     * and the startFrom of the range is satisfiable
194:                     * would return range from file respond with not-modified
195:                     */
196:                    res = newFixedLengthResponse(NOT_MODIFIED, MIME_OCTET_STREAM, "");
197:                    res.addHeader("ETag", etag);
198:
199:                } else {
200:                    if (endAt < 0)
201:                        endAt = stream.fileSize - 1;
202:                    long newLen = endAt - startFrom + 1;
203:                    if (newLen < 0)
204:                        newLen = 0;
205:
206:                    TorrentInputStream is = engine.getTorrentInputStream(stream);
207:                    is.skip(startFrom);
208:
209:                    res = newFixedLengthResponse(PARTIAL_CONTENT, MIME_OCTET_STREAM, is, newLen);
210:                    res.addHeader("Accept-Ranges", "bytes");
211:                    res.addHeader("Content-Length", "" + newLen);
212:                    res.addHeader("Content-Range", "bytes " + startFrom + "-" + endAt + "/" + stream.fileSize);
213:                    res.addHeader("ETag", etag);
214:                    res.addHeader("Content-Disposition", "inline; filename=" + stream.id);
215:                }
216:            } else {
217:                if (headerIfRangeMissingOrMatching && range != null && startFrom >= stream.fileSize) {
218:                    /*
219:                     * Return the size of the file
220:                     * 4xx responses are not trumped by if-none-match
221:                     */
222:                    res = newFixedLengthResponse(RANGE_NOT_SATISFIABLE, NanoHTTPD.MIME_PLAINTEXT, "");
223:                    res.addHeader("Content-Range", "bytes */" + stream.fileSize);
224:                    res.addHeader("ETag", etag);
225:
226:                } else if (range == null && headerIfNoneMatchPresentAndMatching) {
227:                    /*
228:                     * Full-file-fetch request would return entire file
229:                     * respond with not-modified
230:                     */
231:                    res = newFixedLengthResponse(NOT_MODIFIED, MIME_OCTET_STREAM, "");
232:                    res.addHeader("ETag", etag);
233:
234:                } else if (!headerIfRangeMissingOrMatching && headerIfNoneMatchPresentAndMatching) {
235:                    /*
236:                     * Range request that doesn't match current etag
237:                     * would return entire (different) file respond with not-modified
238:                     */
239:                    res = newFixedLengthResponse(NOT_MODIFIED, MIME_OCTET_STREAM, "");
240:                    res.addHeader("ETag", etag);
241:
242:                } else {
243:                    TorrentInputStream is = engine.getTorrentInputStream(stream);
244:                    res = newFixedLengthResponse(OK, MIME_OCTET_STREAM, is, stream.fileSize);
245:                    res.addHeader("Accept-Ranges", "bytes");
246:                    res.addHeader("Content-Length", "" + stream.fileSize);
247:                    res.addHeader("ETag", etag);
248:                    res.addHeader("Content-Disposition", "inline; filename=" + stream.id);
249:                }
250:            }
251:
252:            return res;

29:    public MediaRss parse()
30:    {
31:        MediaRssParser parser = new MediaRssParser(item);
32:
33:        List<Content> content = parser.parseContent();
34:        Hash hash = parser.parseHash();

58:    public Hash parseHash() {
59:        Element mediaElement = item.getElement(MEDIA_RSS_HASH);
60:        if (mediaElement == null || !isMediaRssElement(mediaElement))
61:            return null;
62:
63:        Attributes attr = mediaElement.getAttributes();
64:        String hash = mediaElement.getContent();
65:        if (hash == null)
66:            return null;
67:
68:        Hash mediaHash = new Hash(hash);
69:        String algo = attr.getValue("algo");
70:        if (algo != null)
71:            mediaHash.setAlgorithm(algo);
72:
73:        return mediaHash;
74:    }
75:
76:    public List<PeerLink> parsePeerLinks() {
77:        ArrayList<PeerLink> peerLinks = new ArrayList<PeerLink>();
78:        List<Element> mediaElems = item.getElementList(MEDIA_RSS_PEER_LINK);
79:
80:        for (Element mediaElement : mediaElems) {
81:            if (!isMediaRssElement(mediaElement))
82:                continue;
83:
84:            Attributes attr = mediaElement.getAttributes();
85:            String href = attr.getValue("href");
86:            String type = attr.getValue("type");
87:
88:            peerLinks.add(new PeerLink(href, type));
89:        }
90:
91:        return peerLinks;
92:    }

89:    public List<FeedItem> getItems()
90:    {
91:        List<FeedItem> items = new ArrayList<>();
92:        if (feed == null)
93:            return items;
94:
95:        for (Item item : feed.getItemList()) {

100:            /* Find url with torrent/magnet */
101:            if (downloadUrl == null)
102:                downloadUrl = findDownloadUrl(item);
103:
104:            Date pubDate = item.getPubDate();
105:            long pubDateTime = 0;
106:            if (pubDate != null)
107:                pubDateTime = pubDate.getTime();

141:    private String findDownloadUrl(Item item)
142:    {
143:        /*
144:         * Watch tags in descending order of importance
145:         * (hashes in the last place)
146:         */
147:        String enclosureUrl = watchEnclosure(item);
148:        if (enclosureUrl != null)
149:            return enclosureUrl;
150:
151:        String infoHash = watchTorrentInfoHash(item);
152:        if (infoHash != null)
153:            return infoHash;
154:
155:        String mediaContentUrl = watchMediaContent(item);
156:        if (mediaContentUrl != null)
157:            return mediaContentUrl;
158:
159:        String guid = watchGuid(item);
160:        return guid;
161:    }
162:
163:    /*
164:     * Parse element like this:
165:     * <enclosure type="application/x-bittorrent" url="http://foo.com/bar.torrent"/>
166:     */
167:
168:    private String watchEnclosure(Item item)
169:    {
170:        for (Enclosure enclosure : item.getEnclosures()) {
171:            if (enclosure == null)
172:                continue;
173:
174:            String url = enclosure.getUrl();
175:            String type = enclosure.getType();
176:            if (isMagnetOrTorrent(url) || (type != null && type.equals(Utils.MIME_TORRENT)))
177:                return url;
178:        }
179:
180:        return null;
181:    }
182:
183:    /*
184:     * Parse element like this:
185:     * <media:content type="application/x-bittorrent" url="http://foo.com/bar.torrent"/>
186:     */
187:
188:    private String watchMediaContent(Item item)
189:    {
190:        MediaRss media = item.getMediaRss();
191:        if (media == null)
192:            return null;
193:
194:        List<Content> contentList = media.getContent();
195:        for (Content content : contentList) {
196:            if (content == null)
197:                continue;
198:
199:            String url = content.getUrl();
200:            if (url == null)
201:                return watchMediaHash(media);
202:            String type = content.getType();
203:            if (isMagnetOrTorrent(url) || (type != null && type.equals(Utils.MIME_TORRENT)))
204:                return url;
205:        }
206:
207:        return watchMediaHash(media);
208:    }
209:
210:    /*
211:     * Parse element like this:
212:     * <media:hash algo="sha1">8c056e06fbc16d2a2be79cefbf3e4ddc15396abe</media:hash>
213:     */
214:
215:    private String watchMediaHash(MediaRss media)
216:    {
217:        Hash hash = media.getHash();
218:        if (hash == null)
219:            return null;
220:
221:        String hashStr = hash.getValue();
222:        if (hashStr == null)
223:            return null;
224:        String algo = hash.getAlgorithm();
225:        if (Utils.isHash(hashStr) && algo != null && algo.equalsIgnoreCase("sha1"))
226:            return Utils.normalizeMagnetHash(hashStr);
227:
228:        return null;
229:    }

175:    @Override
176:    public int onStartCommand(Intent intent, int flags, int startId)
177:    {
178:        String action = null;
179:        if (intent != null)
180:            action = intent.getAction();
181:
182:        /* Handle shutdown actions before init service */
183:        if (action != null) {
184:            int ret = handleShutdownActions(action);
185:            if (ret >= 0)
186:                return ret;
187:        }
188:
189:        /* The first start */
190:        if (isAlreadyRunning.compareAndSet(false, true))
191:            init();
192:
193:        if (action != null)
194:            handleActions(action);
195:
196:        return START_STICKY;
197:    }

203:    private void refreshChannel(long feedId)
204:    {

206:            return;

209:                .putString(FeedFetcherWorker.TAG_ACTION, FeedFetcherWorker.ACTION_FETCH_CHANNEL)
210:                .putLong(FeedFetcherWorker.TAG_CHANNEL_ID, feedId)
211:                .putBoolean(FeedFetcherWorker.TAG_NO_AUTO_DOWNLOAD, mutableParams.isNotDownloadImmediately())
212:                .build();
213:
214:        OneTimeWorkRequest work = new OneTimeWorkRequest.Builder(FeedFetcherWorker.class)
215:                .setInputData(data)
216:                .build();
217:
218:        WorkManager.getInstance(getApplication()).enqueue(work);
219:    }

426:    public static void startServiceBackground(@NonNull Context context, @NonNull Intent i) {
427:        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.O) {
428:            // Postpone the service start until the main thread is free to avoid ANR
429:            new Handler(Looper.getMainLooper())
430:                    .post(() -> context.startForegroundService(i));
431:        } else {
432:            context.startService(i);
433:        }
434:    }

1346:                    final InputStream inputStream = finalAccept.getInputStream();
1347:                    NanoHTTPD.this.asyncRunner.exec(createClientHandler(finalAccept, inputStream));
1348:                } catch (IOException e) {
1349:                    NanoHTTPD.LOG.log(Level.FINE, "Communication with the client broken", e);
1350:                }
1351:            } while (!NanoHTTPD.this.myServerSocket.isClosed());
1352:        }
1353:    }

1803:    /**
1804:     * Start the server.
1805:     *
1806:     * @param timeout
1807:     *            timeout to use for socket connections.
1808:     * @param daemon
1809:     *            start the thread daemon or not.
1810:     * @throws IOException
1811:     *             if the socket is in use.
1812:     */
1813:    public void start(final int timeout, boolean daemon) throws IOException {
1814:        this.myServerSocket = this.getServerSocketFactory().create();
1815:        this.myServerSocket.setReuseAddress(true);
1816:        ServerRunnable serverRunnable = createServerRunnable(timeout);
1817:        this.myThread = new Thread(serverRunnable);
1818:        this.myThread.setDaemon(daemon);
1819:        this.myThread.setName("NanoHttpd Main Listener");
1820:        this.myThread.start();
1821:        while (!serverRunnable.hasBinded && serverRunnable.bindException == null) {
1822:            try {
1823:                Thread.sleep(10L);
1824:            } catch (Throwable e) {
1825:                // on android this may not be allowed, that's why we
1826:                // catch throwable the wait should be very short because we are
1827:                // just waiting for the bind of the socket
1828:            }
1829:        }
1830:        if (serverRunnable.bindException != null) {
1831:            throw serverRunnable.bindException;
1832:        }
1833:    }
1834:    /**
1835:     * Stop the server.
1836:     */
1837:    public void stop() {
1838:        try {
1839:            safeClose(this.myServerSocket);
1840:            this.asyncRunner.closeAll();
1841:            if (this.myThread != null) {
1842:                this.myThread.join();
1843:            }
1844:        } catch (Exception e) {
1845:            NanoHTTPD.LOG.log(Level.SEVERE, "Could not stop all connections", e);
1846:        }
1847:    }
1848:    public final boolean wasStarted() {
1849:        return this.myServerSocket != null && this.myThread != null;
1850:    }
$ grep -n '' NanoHTTPD.java | sed -n '154,222p'
154:     * The runnable that will be used for every new client connection.
155:     */
156:    public class ClientHandler implements Runnable {
157:        private final InputStream inputStream;
158:        private final Socket acceptSocket;
159:        private ClientHandler(InputStream inputStream, Socket acceptSocket) {
160:            this.inputStream = inputStream;
161:            this.acceptSocket = acceptSocket;
162:        }
163:        public void close() {
164:            safeClose(this.inputStream);
165:            safeClose(this.acceptSocket);
166:        }
167:        @Override
168:        public void run() {
169:            OutputStream outputStream = null;
170:            try {
171:                outputStream = this.acceptSocket.getOutputStream();
172:                TempFileManager tempFileManager = NanoHTTPD.this.tempFileManagerFactory.create();
173:                HTTPSession session = new HTTPSession(tempFileManager, this.inputStream, outputStream, this.acceptSocket.getInetAddress());
174:                while (!this.acceptSocket.isClosed()) {
175:                    session.execute();
176:                }
177:            } catch (Exception e) {
178:                // When the socket is closed by the client,
179:                // we throw our own SocketException
180:                // to break the "keep alive" loop above. If
181:                // the exception was anything other
182:                // than the expected SocketException OR a
183:                // SocketTimeoutException, print the
184:                // stacktrace
185:                if (!(e instanceof SocketException && "NanoHttpd Shutdown".equals(e.getMessage())) && !(e instanceof SocketTimeoutException)) {
186:                    NanoHTTPD.LOG.log(Level.FINE, "Communication with the client broken", e);
187:                }
188:            } finally {
189:                safeClose(outputStream);
190:                safeClose(this.inputStream);
191:                safeClose(this.acceptSocket);
192:                NanoHTTPD.this.asyncRunner.closed(this);
193:            }
194:        }
195:    }
196:    public static class Cookie {
197:        public static String getHTTPTime(int days) {
198:            Calendar calendar = Calendar.getInstance();
199:            SimpleDateFormat dateFormat = new SimpleDateFormat("EEE, dd MMM yyyy HH:mm:ss z", Locale.US);
200:            dateFormat.setTimeZone(TimeZone.getTimeZone("GMT"));
201:            calendar.add(Calendar.DAY_OF_MONTH, days);
202:            return dateFormat.format(calendar.getTime());
203:        }
204:        private final String n, v, e;
205:        public Cookie(String name, String value) {
206:            this(name, value, 30);
207:        }
208:        public Cookie(String name, String value, int numDays) {
209:            this.n = name;
210:            this.v = value;
211:            this.e = getHTTPTime(numDays);
212:        }
213:        public Cookie(String name, String value, String expires) {
214:            this.n = name;
215:            this.v = value;
216:            this.e = expires;
217:        }
218:        public String getHTTPHeader() {
219:            String fmt = "%s=%s; expires=%s";
220:            return String.format(fmt, this.n, this.v, this.e);
221:        }
222:    }
$ grep -n '' NanoHTTPD.java | sed -n '228,300p;315,338p'
228:     * @author LordFokas
229:     */
230:    public class CookieHandler implements Iterable<String> {
231:        private final HashMap<String, String> cookies = new HashMap<String, String>();
232:        private final ArrayList<Cookie> queue = new ArrayList<Cookie>();
233:        public CookieHandler(Map<String, String> httpHeaders) {
234:            String raw = httpHeaders.get("cookie");
235:            if (raw != null) {
236:                String[] tokens = raw.split(";");
237:                for (String token : tokens) {
238:                    String[] data = token.trim().split("=");
239:                    if (data.length == 2) {
240:                        this.cookies.put(data[0], data[1]);
241:                    }
242:                }
243:            }
244:        }
245:        /**
246:         * Set a cookie with an expiration date from a month ago, effectively
247:         * deleting it on the client side.
248:         *
249:         * @param name
250:         *            The cookie name.
251:         */
252:        public void delete(String name) {
253:            set(name, "-delete-", -30);
254:        }
255:        @NonNull
256:        @Override
257:        public Iterator<String> iterator() {
258:            return this.cookies.keySet().iterator();
259:        }
260:        /**
261:         * Read a cookie from the HTTP Headers.
262:         *
263:         * @param name
264:         *            The cookie's name.
265:         * @return The cookie's value if it exists, null otherwise.
266:         */
267:        public String read(String name) {
268:            return this.cookies.get(name);
269:        }
270:        public void set(Cookie cookie) {
271:            this.queue.add(cookie);
272:        }
273:        /**
274:         * Sets a cookie.
275:         *
276:         * @param name
277:         *            The cookie's name.
278:         * @param value
279:         *            The cookie's value.
280:         * @param expires
281:         *            How many days until the cookie expires.
282:         */
283:        public void set(String name, String value, int expires) {
284:            this.queue.add(new Cookie(name, value, Cookie.getHTTPTime(expires)));
285:        }
286:        /**
287:         * Internally used by the webserver to add all queued cookies into the
288:         * Response's HTTP Headers.
289:         *
290:         * @param response
291:         *            The Response object to which headers the queued cookies
292:         *            will be added.
293:         */
294:        public void unloadQueue(Response response) {
295:            for (Cookie cookie : this.queue) {
296:                response.addHeader("Set-Cookie", cookie.getHTTPHeader());
297:            }
298:        }
299:    }
300:    /**
315:        public List<ClientHandler> getRunning() {
316:            return running;
317:        }
318:        @Override
319:        public void closeAll() {
320:            // copy of the list for concurrency
321:            for (ClientHandler clientHandler : new ArrayList<ClientHandler>(this.running)) {
322:                clientHandler.close();
323:            }
324:        }
325:        @Override
326:        public void closed(ClientHandler clientHandler) {
327:            this.running.remove(clientHandler);
328:        }
329:        @Override
330:        public void exec(ClientHandler clientHandler) {
331:            ++this.requestCount;
332:            Thread t = new Thread(clientHandler);
333:            t.setDaemon(true);
334:            t.setName("NanoHttpd Request Processor (#" + this.requestCount + ")");
335:            this.running.add(clientHandler);
336:            t.start();
337:        }
338:    }

1:package com.ernieyu.feedparser.mediarss;
2:
3:import androidx.annotation.NonNull;
4:
5:/**
6: * This is the hash of the binary media file.
7: * It can appear multiple times as long as each instance is a different algo.
8: * It has one optional attribute.
9: *
10: * <media:hash algo="md5">dfdec888b72151965a34b4b59031290a</media:hash>
11: *
12: * algo indicates the algorithm used to create the hash.
13: * Possible values are "md5" and "sha-1". Default value is "md5".
14: * It is an optional attribute.
15: */
16:
17:public class Hash {
18:    private String value;
19:    private String algorithm = "md5";
20:
21:    public Hash(String value) {
22:        this.value = value;
23:    }
24:
25:    /**
26:     * Returns a hash.
27:     * <p>
28:     *
29:     * @return a hash, <b>null</b> if none.
30:     */
31:
32:    public String getValue() {
33:        return value;
34:    }
35:
36:    /**
37:     * Returns the hash algorithm.
38:     * <p>
39:     *
40:     * @return the hash algorithm.
41:     * Possible values are "md5" and "sha-1". Default value is "md5".
42:     */
43:
44:    public String getAlgorithm() {
45:        return algorithm;
46:    }
47:
48:    public void setAlgorithm(String algorithm) {
49:        if (algorithm == null)
50:            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Algorithm cannot be null");
51:        this.algorithm = algorithm;
52:    }
53:
54:    @Override
55:    public boolean equals(Object o) {
56:        if (this == o) return true;
57:        if (o == null || getClass() != o.getClass()) return false;
58:
59:        Hash that = (Hash) o;
60:
61:        if (value != null ? !value.equals(that.value) : that.value != null)
62:            return false;
63:        return algorithm != null ? algorithm.equals(that.algorithm) : that.algorithm == null;
64:    }
65:
66:    @Override
67:    public int hashCode() {
68:        int result = value != null ? value.hashCode() : 0;
69:        result = 31 * result + (algorithm != null ? algorithm.hashCode() : 0);
70:        return result;
71:    }
72:
73:    @NonNull
74:    @Override
75:    public String toString() {
76:        return "MediaRssHash{" +
77:            "value='" + value + '\'' +
78:            ", algorithm='" + algorithm + '\'' +
79:            '}';
80:    }
81:}

$ grep -n '' FeedParser.java | sed -n '89,116p;141,161p;188,229p'
89:    public List<FeedItem> getItems()
90:    {
91:        List<FeedItem> items = new ArrayList<>();
92:        if (feed == null)
93:            return items;
94:
95:        for (Item item : feed.getItemList()) {
96:            List<String> links = item.getLinks();
97:            String articleUrl = getFirstNotNullLink(links);
98:            String downloadUrl = watchDownloadableLink(links);
99:
100:            /* Find url with torrent/magnet */
101:            if (downloadUrl == null)
102:                downloadUrl = findDownloadUrl(item);
103:
104:            Date pubDate = item.getPubDate();
105:            long pubDateTime = 0;
106:            if (pubDate != null)
107:                pubDateTime = pubDate.getTime();
108:
109:            FeedItem feedItem = new FeedItem(feedChannel.id, downloadUrl,
110:                    articleUrl, item.getTitle(), pubDateTime);
111:            feedItem.fetchDate = System.currentTimeMillis();
112:            items.add(feedItem);
113:        }
114:
115:        return items;
116:    }
141:    private String findDownloadUrl(Item item)
142:    {
143:        /*
144:         * Watch tags in descending order of importance
145:         * (hashes in the last place)
146:         */
147:        String enclosureUrl = watchEnclosure(item);
148:        if (enclosureUrl != null)
149:            return enclosureUrl;
150:
151:        String infoHash = watchTorrentInfoHash(item);
152:        if (infoHash != null)
153:            return infoHash;
154:
155:        String mediaContentUrl = watchMediaContent(item);
156:        if (mediaContentUrl != null)
157:            return mediaContentUrl;
158:
159:        String guid = watchGuid(item);
160:        return guid;
161:    }
188:    private String watchMediaContent(Item item)
189:    {
190:        MediaRss media = item.getMediaRss();
191:        if (media == null)
192:            return null;
193:
194:        List<Content> contentList = media.getContent();
195:        for (Content content : contentList) {
196:            if (content == null)
197:                continue;
198:
199:            String url = content.getUrl();
200:            if (url == null)
201:                return watchMediaHash(media);
202:            String type = content.getType();
203:            if (isMagnetOrTorrent(url) || (type != null && type.equals(Utils.MIME_TORRENT)))
204:                return url;
205:        }
206:
207:        return watchMediaHash(media);
208:    }
209:
210:    /*
211:     * Parse element like this:
212:     * <media:hash algo="sha1">8c056e06fbc16d2a2be79cefbf3e4ddc15396abe</media:hash>
213:     */
214:
215:    private String watchMediaHash(MediaRss media)
216:    {
217:        Hash hash = media.getHash();
218:        if (hash == null)
219:            return null;
220:
221:        String hashStr = hash.getValue();
222:        if (hashStr == null)
223:            return null;
224:        String algo = hash.getAlgorithm();
225:        if (Utils.isHash(hashStr) && algo != null && algo.equalsIgnoreCase("sha1"))
226:            return Utils.normalizeMagnetHash(hashStr);
227:
228:        return null;
229:    }
$ grep -n '' MediaRssParser.java | sed -n '25,92p'
25:    public MediaRssParser(Rss2Item item) {
26:        this.item = item;
27:    }
28:
29:    public MediaRss parse()
30:    {
31:        MediaRssParser parser = new MediaRssParser(item);
32:
33:        List<Content> content = parser.parseContent();
34:        Hash hash = parser.parseHash();
35:        List<PeerLink> peerLinks = parser.parsePeerLinks();
36:
37:        return new MediaRss(content, hash, peerLinks);
38:    }
39:
40:    public List<Content> parseContent() {
41:        ArrayList<Content> content = new ArrayList<Content>();
42:        List<Element> mediaElems = item.getElementList(MEDIA_RSS_CONTENT);
43:
44:        for (Element mediaElement : mediaElems) {
45:            if (!isMediaRssElement(mediaElement))
46:                continue;
47:
48:            Attributes attr = mediaElement.getAttributes();
49:            String url = attr.getValue("url");
50:            String type = attr.getValue("type");
51:
52:            content.add(new Content(url, type));
53:        }
54:
55:        return content;
56:    }
57:
58:    public Hash parseHash() {
59:        Element mediaElement = item.getElement(MEDIA_RSS_HASH);
60:        if (mediaElement == null || !isMediaRssElement(mediaElement))
61:            return null;
62:
63:        Attributes attr = mediaElement.getAttributes();
64:        String hash = mediaElement.getContent();
65:        if (hash == null)
66:            return null;
67:
68:        Hash mediaHash = new Hash(hash);
69:        String algo = attr.getValue("algo");
70:        if (algo != null)
71:            mediaHash.setAlgorithm(algo);
72:
73:        return mediaHash;
74:    }
75:
76:    public List<PeerLink> parsePeerLinks() {
77:        ArrayList<PeerLink> peerLinks = new ArrayList<PeerLink>();
78:        List<Element> mediaElems = item.getElementList(MEDIA_RSS_PEER_LINK);
79:
80:        for (Element mediaElement : mediaElems) {
81:            if (!isMediaRssElement(mediaElement))
82:                continue;
83:
84:            Attributes attr = mediaElement.getAttributes();
85:            String href = attr.getValue("href");
86:            String type = attr.getValue("type");
87:
88:            peerLinks.add(new PeerLink(href, type));
89:        }
90:
91:        return peerLinks;
92:    }

1699:    /**
1700:     * Create a text response with known length.
1701:     */
1702:    public static Response newFixedLengthResponse(Response.IStatus status, String mimeType, String txt) {
1703:        if (txt == null) {
1704:            return newFixedLengthResponse(status, mimeType, new ByteArrayInputStream(new byte[0]), 0);
1705:        } else {
1706:            byte[] bytes;
1707:            try {
1708:                bytes = txt.getBytes("UTF-8");
1709:            } catch (UnsupportedEncodingException e) {
1710:                NanoHTTPD.LOG.log(Level.SEVERE, "encoding problem, responding nothing", e);
1711:                bytes = new byte[0];
1712:            }
1713:            return newFixedLengthResponse(status, mimeType, new ByteArrayInputStream(bytes), bytes.length);
1714:        }

    24	     * @param timeout
    25	     *            timeout to use for socket connections.

    31	    public void start(final int timeout, boolean daemon) throws IOException {
    32	        this.myServerSocket = this.getServerSocketFactory().create();
    33	        this.myServerSocket.setReuseAddress(true);
    34	        ServerRunnable serverRunnable = createServerRunnable(timeout);
    35	        this.myThread = new Thread(serverRunnable);
    36	        this.myThread.setDaemon(daemon);
    37	        this.myThread.setName("NanoHttpd Main Listener");

    40	            try {
    41	                Thread.sleep(10L);
    42	            } catch (Throwable e) {
    43	                // on android this may not be allowed, that's why we
    44	                // catch throwable the wait should be very short because we are
    45	                // just waiting for the bind of the socket
    46	            }
    47	        }
    48	        if (serverRunnable.bindException != null) {
    49	            throw serverRunnable.bindException;
    50	        }
    51	    }

     1	    public class ClientHandler implements Runnable {
     2	        private final InputStream inputStream;
     3	        private final Socket acceptSocket;
     4	        private ClientHandler(InputStream inputStream, Socket acceptSocket) {
     5	            this.inputStream = inputStream;
     6	            this.acceptSocket = acceptSocket;
     7	        }
     8	        public void close() {
     9	            safeClose(this.inputStream);
    10	            safeClose(this.acceptSocket);
    11	        }
    12	        @Override
    13	        public void run() {
    14	            OutputStream outputStream = null;
    15	            try {
    16	                outputStream = this.acceptSocket.getOutputStream();
    17	                TempFileManager tempFileManager = NanoHTTPD.this.tempFileManagerFactory.create();
    18	                HTTPSession session = new HTTPSession(tempFileManager, this.inputStream, outputStream, this.acceptSocket.getInetAddress());
    19	                while (!this.acceptSocket.isClosed()) {
    20	                    session.execute();
    21	                }
    22	            } catch (Exception e) {
    23	                // When the socket is closed by the client,
    24	                // we throw our own SocketException
    25	                // to break the "keep alive" loop above. If
    26	                // the exception was anything other
    27	                // than the expected SocketException OR a
    28	                // SocketTimeoutException, print the
    29	                // stacktrace
    30	                if (!(e instanceof SocketException && "NanoHttpd Shutdown".equals(e.getMessage())) && !(e instanceof SocketTimeoutException)) {
    31	                    NanoHTTPD.LOG.log(Level.FINE, "Communication with the client broken", e);
    32	                }
    33	            } finally {
    34	                safeClose(outputStream);
    35	                safeClose(this.inputStream);
    36	                safeClose(this.acceptSocket);
    37	                NanoHTTPD.this.asyncRunner.closed(this);
    38	            }
    39	        }
    40	    }
    41	    public static class Cookie {
    42	            return running;
    43	        }
    44	        @Override
    45	        public void closeAll() {
    46	            // copy of the list for concurrency
    47	            for (ClientHandler clientHandler : new ArrayList<ClientHandler>(this.running)) {
    48	                clientHandler.close();
    49	            }
    50	        }
    51	        @Override
    52	        public void closed(ClientHandler clientHandler) {
    53	            this.running.remove(clientHandler);
    54	        }
    55	        @Override
    56	        public void exec(ClientHandler clientHandler) {
    57	            ++this.requestCount;
    58	            Thread t = new Thread(clientHandler);
    59	            t.setDaemon(true);
    60	            t.setName("NanoHttpd Request Processor (#" + this.requestCount + ")");
    61	            this.running.add(clientHandler);
    62	            t.start();
    63	        }
    64	    }

145:

$ awk NanoHTTPD.java 196-300 (Cookie, CookieHandler) 
196:     public static class Cookie {
197:         public static String getHTTPTime(int days) {
198:             Calendar calendar = Calendar.getInstance();
199:             SimpleDateFormat dateFormat = new SimpleDateFormat("EEE, dd MMM yyyy HH:mm:ss z", Locale.US);
200:             dateFormat.setTimeZone(TimeZone.getTimeZone("GMT"));
201:             calendar.add(Calendar.DAY_OF_MONTH, days);
202:             return dateFormat.format(calendar.getTime());
203:         }
204:         private final String n, v, e;
205:         public Cookie(String name, String value) {
206:             this(name, value, 30);
207:         }
208:         public Cookie(String name, String value, int numDays) {
209:             this.n = name;
210:             this.v = value;
211:             this.e = getHTTPTime(numDays);
212:         }
213:         public Cookie(String name, String value, String expires) {
214:             this.n = name;
215:             this.v = value;
216:             this.e = expires;
217:         }
218:         public String getHTTPHeader() {
219:             String fmt = "%s=%s; expires=%s";
220:             return String.format(fmt, this.n, this.v, this.e);
221:         }
222:     }
223:     /**
224:      * Provides rudimentary support for cookies. Doesn't support 'path',
225:      * 'secure' nor 'httpOnly'. Feel free to improve it and/or add unsupported
226:      * features.
227:      *
228:      * @author LordFokas
229:      */
230:     public class CookieHandler implements Iterable<String> {
231:         private final HashMap<String, String> cookies = new HashMap<String, String>();
232:         private final ArrayList<Cookie> queue = new ArrayList<Cookie>();
233:         public CookieHandler(Map<String, String> httpHeaders) {
234:             String raw = httpHeaders.get("cookie");
